# Save-game command reconstructions

For every `.sav` file these transcripts have evidence for, the full sequence of player commands (no `save`/`restore`/`restart`/`quit` or debug `#` commands) needed to reach that save's exact state, starting from the true beginning of its session. Regenerated by `rebuild_save_artifacts.py`, which auto-discovers every `transcript*.txt` in this directory and auto-orders them by restore-dependency: a transcript that opens with `restore <name>` is chained after whichever transcript(s) produced `<name>`; independent play sessions (no shared save history) become separate groups below.
